- **qu-brats-score**: Evaluates the calibration of voxel-wise uncertainty estimates in brain tumor segmentation. Use when the user has predictions and gold and needs to compute QU-BraTS unified score. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/qu-brats-score) · ★3)
